<h2 id="Welcome_to_the_community_portal!" data-mw-thread-id="h-Welcome_to_the_community_portal!" class="mw-html-heading"><span data-mw-comment-start="" id="h-Welcome_to_the_community_portal!"></span><span id="Welcome_to_the_community_portal.21" typeof="mw:FallbackId"></span>
Welcome to the community portal!
<span data-mw-comment-end="h-Welcome_to_the_community_portal!"></span></h2><!-- 3 line spacing of h2 is important for subsection edit links to work correctly, PLEASE DO NOT FIX -->

<span class="mw-empty-elt" about="#mwt13" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwDg" <p id="mwEA">This page provides a listing of current collaborations, tasks, and news about English Wikipedia. New to <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Wikipedia" title="Wikipedia" id="mwEQ">Wikipedia</a>? See the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Wikipedia:Contributing_to_Wikipedia" title="Wikipedia:Contributing to Wikipedia" id="mwEg">contributing to Wikipedia</a> page or <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Help:Introduction" title="Help:Introduction" id="mwEw">our tutorial</a> for everything you need to know to get started. For a listing of <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Wikipedia:ADMINP" title="Wikipedia:ADMINP" class="mw-redirect" id="mwFA">internal project pages</a> of interest, see the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Wikipedia:Department_directory" title="Wikipedia:Department directory" id="mwFQ">department directory</a>.</p>

<h2 id="Community_bulletin_board" data-mw-thread-id="h-Community_bulletin_board-20260325094600" class="mw-html-heading"><span data-mw-comment-start="" id="h-Community_bulletin_board-20260325094600"></span>
Community bulletin board
<span data-mw-comment-end="h-Community_bulletin_board-20260325094600"></span></h2><!-- 3 line spacing of h2 is important for subsection edit links to work correctly, PLEASE DO NOT FIX
--><span data-mw-comment-start="" id="c-Catfurball-20260901152100-Community_bulletin_board"></span><span class="mw-empty-elt" about="#mwt19" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwTA" data-mw="{&quot;parts&quot;:[{&quot;template&quot;:{&quot;target&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;transcluded section&quot;,&quot;href&quot;:&quot;./Template:Transcluded_section&quot;},&quot;params&quot;:{&quot;source&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;Wikipedia:Community bulletin board&quot;}},&quot;i&quot;:0}}]}"><link rel="mw-deduplicated-inline-style" href="mw-data:TemplateStyles:r1368532237" about="#mwt20" typeof="mw:Extension/templatestyles" data-mw="{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;templatestyles&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{&quot;src&quot;:&quot;Module:Hatnote/styles.css&quot;},&quot;body&quot;:{&quot;extsrc&quot;:&quot;&quot;}}"></span><div role="note" class="hatnote navigation-not-searchable relarticle mainarticle selfreference noprint" about="#mwt19" id="mwTQ">This section is <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Help:Transclusion" title="Help:Transclusion">transcluded</a> from <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Wikipedia:Community_bulletin_board" title="Wikipedia:Community bulletin board">Wikipedia:Community bulletin board</a>. <span class="plainlinks" style="font-style: normal; font-size:85%;">(<a rel="mw:ExtLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/w/index.php?title=Wikipedia:Community_bulletin_board&amp;action=edit" class="external text" data-mw-original-href="//en.wikipedia.org/w/index.php?title=Wikipedia:Community_bulletin_board&amp;action=edit">edit</a> <span typeof="mw:Entity">|</span> <a rel="mw:ExtLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/w/index.php?title=Wikipedia:Community_bulletin_board&amp;action=history" class="external text" data-mw-original-href="//en.wikipedia.org/w/index.php?title=Wikipedia:Community_bulletin_board&amp;action=history">history</a>)</span></div>
<span class="mw-empty-elt" about="#mwt21" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwTg" data-mw="{&quot;parts&quot;:[{&quot;template&quot;:{&quot;target&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;Wikipedia:Community bulletin board&quot;,&quot;href&quot;:&quot;./Wikipedia:Community_bulletin_board&quot;},&quot;params&quot;:{},&quot;i&quot;:0}}]}"></span><table role="presentation" class="mw-collapsible mw-archivedtalk mw-collapsed " style=" text-align: left; border: 1px solid var(--border-color-subtle, #c8ccd1); margin: 0.2em auto auto; width:100%; clear: both; padding: 1px;" about="#mwt21">
<tbody><tr>
<th style="background-color: var(--background-color-interactive, #eaecf0);color:inherit; font-size:87%; padding:0.2em 0.3em; text-align: left; "><div style="display: inline; font-size: 115%; color: var(--color-base, #202122);"><span typeof="mw:File"><span><img resource="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/File:Imbox_notice.png" src="//thumb.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/c/c8/Ambox_notice.png/20px-Ambox_notice.png?utm_source=en.wikipedia.org&amp;utm_campaign=parser&amp;utm_content=thumbnail" decoding="async" loading="lazy" srcset="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/c/c8/Ambox_notice.png?utm_source=en.wikipedia.org&amp;utm_campaign=parser&amp;utm_content=thumbnail_unscaled 2x" alt="" data-file-width="40" data-file-height="40" data-file-type="bitmap" height="16" width="16" class="mw-file-element"></span></span> How to add to the community bulletin board</div></th></tr>
<tr>
<td style="border: solid 1px var(--border-color-subtle, #c8ccd1); padding: 8px; background-color: var(--background-color-base, #fff); color: var(--color-base, #202122);">
<div>
<span class="mw-empty-elt"><link rel="mw-deduplicated-inline-style" href="mw-data:TemplateStyles:r1374733320" about="#mwt22" typeof="mw:Extension/templatestyles" data-mw="{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;templatestyles&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{&quot;src&quot;:&quot;Module:Message box/ombox.css&quot;},&quot;body&quot;:{&quot;extsrc&quot;:&quot;&quot;}}"></span><table class="box-Notice plainlinks ombox ombox-notice" role="presentation"><tbody><tr><td class="mbox-image"><span typeof="mw:File"><span><img resource="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/File:Information_icon4.svg" src="//thumb.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/en/thumb/1/1d/Information_icon4.svg/40px-Information_icon4.svg.png?utm_source=en.wikipedia.org&amp;utm_campaign=parser&amp;utm_content=thumbnail" decoding="async" loading="lazy" srcset="//thumb.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/en/thumb/1/1d/Information_icon4.svg/120px-Information_icon4.svg.png?utm_source=en.wikipedia.org&amp;utm_campaign=parser&amp;utm_content=thumbnail 2x" alt="" data-file-width="620" data-file-height="620" data-file-type="drawing" height="40" width="40" class="mw-file-element"></span></span></td><td class="mbox-text" style="text-align: left;">The community bulletin board has 2 sections that can be used by Wikipedians for announcements: "Events and projects" and "WikiProject notices". In general, keep it <b>concise</b> (under 2 lines), refrain from <b>fancy formatting</b>, and new entries should be placed at the <b>top</b> of their section.
<ul><li>Before editing, make sure you are on <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Wikipedia:Community_bulletin_board" title="Wikipedia:Community bulletin board">Wikipedia:Community bulletin board</a>, not <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Wikipedia:Community_portal" class="mw-selflink selflink">Wikipedia:Community portal</a>, where the board is transcluded.</li>
<li><b>Events and projects</b>: In this section, only organized events, projects, and/or competitions should be listed. These are organized by how often they occur:
<ul><li>The <b>Yearly</b> section is for uncommon events, like events that only occur every year, once, or irregularly. The <b>Monthly</b> section is for events that occur each month, or are always ongoing.</li></ul></li>
<li><b>WikiProject notices</b>: In this section, any announcement, request for help or other notice from a WikiProject should be listed here.
<ul><li>Entries should be signed, and ordered from newest to oldest.</li>
<li>Entries are to be removed after a period of <b>6 months</b>.</li></ul></li></ul></td></tr></tbody></table>
</div></td></tr>
</tbody></table><span about="#mwt21">

</span><p about="#mwt21">Welcome to the <b>community bulletin board</b>, which is a page used for announcements from <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Wikipedia:Wikiproject" title="Wikipedia:Wikiproject" class="mw-redirect">WikiProjects</a> and other groups. Included here are coordinated efforts, events, projects, and other general announcements.</p><span about="#mwt21">

</span><link rel="mw-deduplicated-inline-style" href="mw-data:TemplateStyles:r1374427404" about="#mwt21" typeof="mw:Extension/templatestyles" data-mw="{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;templatestyles&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{&quot;src&quot;:&quot;Hlist/styles.css&quot;}}"><div class="hlist " style=" text-align: center" about="#mwt21">
<ul><li><a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Wikipedia:Dashboard" title="Wikipedia:Dashboard">Dashboard</a></li>
<li><a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Wikipedia:News" title="Wikipedia:News">News</a></li>
<li><a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Wikipedia:Task_Center" title="Wikipedia:Task Center">Task Center</a></li>
<li><a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Wikipedia:Goings-on" title="Wikipedia:Goings-on">Goings-on</a></li>
<li><a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Wikipedia:Meetups" title="Wikipedia:Meetups" class="mw-redirect">Meetups</a></li>
<li><a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Wikipedia:Wikipedia_in_the_media" title="Wikipedia:Wikipedia in the media">In the media</a></li>
<li><a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Wikipedia:Mailing_lists" title="Wikipedia:Mailing lists">Mailing lists</a></li></ul>

<div class="wp-comm-bulletin-box wp-comm-bulletin-events">
<div class="wp-comm-bulletin-header">Events and projects <small><a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Special:EditPage/Wikipedia:Community_bulletin_board" title="Special:EditPage/Wikipedia:Community bulletin board">[add]</a></small></div>

<p><b>Yearly or infrequent events</b></p>
<ul><li><a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Wikipedia:WikiProject_Organized_Labour" title="Wikipedia:WikiProject Organized Labour">WikiProject Organized Labour</a> is helping organise a month-long multi-language editing campaign in solidarity with <a rel="mw:WikiLink/Interwiki" href="https://meta.wikimedia.org/wiki/Wiki%20Workers%20United" title="meta:Wiki Workers United" class="extiw">meta:Wiki Workers United</a>, including about the history of trade union and current developments. <a rel="mw:WikiLink/Interwiki" href="https://meta.wikimedia.org/wiki/Event:WikiProject%20Organized%20Labour%20Month%20Online%20Campaign" title="meta:Event:WikiProject Organized Labour Month Online Campaign" class="extiw">Sign up on meta</a></li></ul>

<p><b>Monthly or continuous events</b></p>
<ul><li><b><a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Wikipedia:WikiProject_Military_history/Contest" title="Wikipedia:WikiProject Military history/Contest">Monthly contest, WikiProject Military history</a>.</b> The contest department of the Military history WikiProject aims to motivate increased quality in military history articles by offering a form of friendly competition for project members making improvements to them. The primary contest available is a simple rolling competition that awards points for improving articles. The contest runs from the first to last day of each month.</li>
<li><b>Guild of Copy Editors: drive</b>.  The <b><a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Wikipedia:WikiProject_Guild_of_Copy_Editors/Backlog_elimination_drives/September_2026" title="Wikipedia:WikiProject Guild of Copy Editors/Backlog elimination drives/September 2026">September 2026 backlog drive</a></b> is a one-month-long effort by the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Wikipedia:WikiProject_Guild_of_Copy_Editors" title="Wikipedia:WikiProject Guild of Copy Editors"><b>Guild of Copy Editors</b></a> to reduce the backlog of articles that require copy editing: those carrying the <span class="nowrap"><span typeof="mw:Entity">{</span><span typeof="mw:Entity">{</span></span><a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Template:Copy_edit" title="Template:Copy edit">copy edit</a><span class="nowrap"><span typeof="mw:Entity">}</span><span typeof="mw:Entity">}</span></span> tag (also <span class="nowrap"><span typeof="mw:Entity">{</span><span typeof="mw:Entity">{</span></span><a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Template:Awkward" title="Template:Awkward" class="mw-redirect">awkward</a><span class="nowrap"><span typeof="mw:Entity">}</span><span typeof="mw:Entity">}</span></span>, <span class="nowrap"><span typeof="mw:Entity">{</span><span typeof="mw:Entity">{</span></span><a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Template:Copy_edit_section" title="Template:Copy edit section">copy edit section</a><span class="nowrap"><span typeof="mw:Entity">}</span><span typeof="mw:Entity">}</span></span>, <span class="nowrap"><span typeof="mw:Entity">{</span><span typeof="mw:Entity">{</span></span><a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Template:Inappropriate_person" title="Template:Inappropriate person">inappropriate person</a><span class="nowrap"><span typeof="mw:Entity">}</span><span typeof="mw:Entity">}</span></span>, <span class="nowrap"><span typeof="mw:Entity">{</span><span typeof="mw:Entity">{</span></span><a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Template:Copy_edit_inline" title="Template:Copy edit inline">copy edit inline</a><span class="nowrap"><span typeof="mw:Entity">}</span><span typeof="mw:Entity">}</span></span>, and their redirects), and those listed on the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Wikipedia:GOCE/REQ" title="Wikipedia:GOCE/REQ" class="mw-redirect">GOCE Requests page</a>. <u>It began on 1 September, 00:00 (UTC), and will end on 30 September, 23:59 (UTC).</u></li>
<li><b><a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Wikipedia:WikiProject_Women_in_Red" title="Wikipedia:WikiProject Women in Red">Wikipedia:WikiProject Women in Red</a> 2025 Events</b>:</li></ul>

<p><i>Also consider posting WikiProject, Task Force, and Collaboration news at </i>The Signpost<i><span class="nowrap"><span typeof="mw:Entity">'</span></span>s <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Wikipedia:Wikipedia_Signpost/Newsroom/WikiProject_desk#Sidebar_news_requests" title="Wikipedia:Wikipedia Signpost/Newsroom/WikiProject desk">WikiProject Report</a> page.</i>
<i>Please include your signature when adding a listing here.</i></p>
<ul><li><a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Wikipedia:WikiProject_Unreferenced_articles" title="Wikipedia:WikiProject Unreferenced articles">WP Unreferenced articles</a> is trying to clear the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Category:Articles_lacking_sources" title="Category:Articles lacking sources">decades-long backlog of <b>unreferenced articles</b></a>, please help to lower the number of unreferenced articles below 30,000. <div class="wp-comm-bulletin-header"><a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Wikipedia:Tech_news" title="Wikipedia:Tech news">Technical news</a></div>

<span class="mw-empty-elt"><link rel="mw-deduplicated-inline-style" href="mw-data:TemplateStyles:r1368532237" about="#mwt25" typeof="mw:Extension/templatestyles" data-mw="{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;templatestyles&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{&quot;src&quot;:&quot;Module:Hatnote/styles.css&quot;},&quot;body&quot;:{&quot;extsrc&quot;:&quot;&quot;}}"></span><div role="note" class="hatnote navigation-not-searchable relarticle mainarticle selfreference noprint">This section is <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Help:Transclusion" title="Help:Transclusion">transcluded</a> from <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Wikipedia:Tech_news" title="Wikipedia:Tech news">Wikipedia:Tech news</a>. <span class="plainlinks" style="font-style: normal; font-size:85%;">(<a rel="mw:ExtLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/w/index.php?title=Wikipedia:Tech_news&amp;action=edit" class="external text" data-mw-original-href="//en.wikipedia.org/w/index.php?title=Wikipedia:Tech_news&amp;action=edit">edit</a> <span typeof="mw:Entity">|</span> <a rel="mw:ExtLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/w/index.php?title=Wikipedia:Tech_news&amp;action=history" class="external text" data-mw-original-href="//en.wikipedia.org/w/index.php?title=Wikipedia:Tech_news&amp;action=history">history</a>)</span></div>
<div class="plainlinks">
<p>Latest <b><a rel="mw:WikiLink/Interwiki" href="https://meta.wikimedia.org/wiki/Special:MyLanguage/Tech/News" title="m:Special:MyLanguage/Tech/News" class="extiw">tech news</a></b> from the Wikimedia technical community. Please tell other users about these changes. Not all changes will affect you. <a rel="mw:WikiLink/Interwiki" href="https://meta.wikimedia.org/wiki/Special:MyLanguage/Tech/News/2026/38" title="m:Special:MyLanguage/Tech/News/2026/38" class="extiw">Translations</a> are available.</p>

<p><b>Weekly highlight</b></p>
<ul><li>The Future Audiences team has started a <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Special:MyLanguage/Wikipedia:Village_pump_(proposals)#New_proposed_experiment_to_let_readers_know_about_“Google_preferred_sources”" title="Special:MyLanguage/Wikipedia:Village pump (proposals)">discussion on English Wikipedia</a> about a proposed new experiment to show a temporary notice about the new <a rel="mw:ExtLink nofollow" href="https://blog.google/products-and-platforms/products/search/original-high-quality-content-search/" class="external text">Google Preferred Sources</a> feature to Wikipedia readers coming from Google. The experiment is to test whether this notice makes visitors come back to Wikipedia more often. Preferred Sources feature lets users choose websites they trust so Google can highlight content from those sources more prominently in Search and AI experiences. We are interested in testing this on other languages that Google supports and would welcome assistance with starting conversations on other wikis. If interested, please reach out to Future Audiences on the <a rel="mw:WikiLink/Interwiki" href="https://meta.wikimedia.org/wiki/Special:MyLanguage/Future%20Audiences/Preferred%20sources" title="m:Special:MyLanguage/Future Audiences/Preferred sources" class="extiw">talk page</a>.</li></ul>

<p><b>Updates for editors</b></p>
<ul><li>The chat platform Discord is releasing a new self-service framework that websites can use to specify how their links should look when shared on Discord. The Future Audiences team is planning to start using this new framework to improve how Wikipedia links look when shared on Discord, giving better attribution and credit to contributors. The link appearance and behavior won't change in the first phase of this project as we want to first collect some baseline data to assess the impact of future changes. However, if community members who are active on Discord spot any issues, they can reach out on <a rel="mw:WikiLink/Interwiki" href="https://phabricator.wikimedia.org/tag/future-audiences/" title="phab:tag/future-audiences/" class="extiw">Phabricator</a> or <a rel="mw:WikiLink/Interwiki" href="https://meta.wikimedia.org/wiki/Special:MyLanguage/Future%20Audiences" title="m:Special:MyLanguage/Future Audiences" class="extiw">Metawiki</a>.</li>
<li>The Reader Growth team will be running an experiment to test whether <a rel="mw:WikiLink/Interwiki" href="https://www.mediawiki.org/wiki/Special:MyLanguage/Readers/Reader_Growth/Compact%20Lead" title="mw:Special:MyLanguage/Readers/Reader Growth/Compact Lead" class="extiw">compacting article lead sections on mobiles</a> with the addition of a "read more" button improves reader retention. The test will begin on September 17 on Arabic, Spanish, French, Indonesian, Italian, Japanese, Portuguese, Vietnamese, and Chinese Wikipedias and will run for four weeks.</li>
<li>All wikis will be <a rel="mw:WikiLink/Interwiki" href="https://meta.wikimedia.org/wiki/Special:MyLanguage/Tech/Server%20switch" title="m:Special:MyLanguage/Tech/Server switch" class="extiw">read-only for a few minutes</a> on September 23. This is planned at 14:00 UTC. More information will be published in Tech News and will also be posted on individual wikis in the coming week. <a rel="mw:ExtLink" href="https://phabricator.wikimedia.org/T433363" class="external autonumber"></a></li>
<li><span class="skin-invert" typeof="mw:File" data-mw="{&quot;caption&quot;:&quot;Recurrent item&quot;}"><span title="Recurrent item"><img resource="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/File:Reload_icon_with_two_arrows.svg" src="//thumb.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/2/2e/Reload_icon_with_two_arrows.svg/20px-Reload_icon_with_two_arrows.svg.png?utm_source=en.wikipedia.org&amp;utm_campaign=parser&amp;utm_content=thumbnail" decoding="async" loading="lazy" srcset="//thumb.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/2/2e/Reload_icon_with_two_arrows.svg/40px-Reload_icon_with_two_arrows.svg.png?utm_source=en.wikipedia.org&amp;utm_campaign=parser&amp;utm_content=thumbnail 2x" alt="Recurrent item" data-file-width="512" data-file-height="512" data-file-type="drawing" height="12" width="12" class="mw-file-element"></span></span> View all 28 community-submitted tasks that were <a rel="mw:WikiLink/Interwiki" href="https://meta.wikimedia.org/wiki/Special:MyLanguage/Tech/News/Recently%20resolved%20community%20tasks" title="m:Special:MyLanguage/Tech/News/Recently resolved community tasks" class="extiw">resolved last week</a>. For example, an issue where <a rel="mw:ExtLink nofollow" href="https://wikistats.wmcloud.org/" class="external text">Wikistats</a> for Wikipedias was returning an HTTP 500 error and could not be reached, has now been fixed. <a rel="mw:ExtLink" href="https://phabricator.wikimedia.org/T435959" class="external autonumber"></a></li></ul>

<p><b>Updates for technical contributors</b></p>
<ul><li>Developers who maintain a tool that queries the Wikimedia Commons links tables need to update their code to connect to the new x4 database cluster. The links tables have been moved from the s4 cluster to x4, and will no longer receive updates on s4. The page and redirect tables remain available on both clusters. A wiki replica for the x4 cluster will be set up afterwards. The change is being made because the s4 cluster has grown too large to operate efficiently. You can <a rel="mw:WikiLink/Interwiki" href="https://wikitech.wikimedia.org/wiki/Special:MyLanguage/News/2026%20Commons%20links%20tables%20database%20split" title="wikitech:Special:MyLanguage/News/2026 Commons links tables database split" class="extiw">read more</a>.</li>
<li><span class="skin-invert" typeof="mw:File" data-mw="{&quot;caption&quot;:&quot;Recurrent item&quot;}"><span title="Recurrent item"><img resource="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/File:Reload_icon_with_two_arrows.svg" src="//thumb.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/2/2e/Reload_icon_with_two_arrows.svg/20px-Reload_icon_with_two_arrows.svg.png?utm_source=en.wikipedia.org&amp;utm_campaign=parser&amp;utm_content=thumbnail" decoding="async" loading="lazy" srcset="//thumb.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/2/2e/Reload_icon_with_two_arrows.svg/40px-Reload_icon_with_two_arrows.svg.png?utm_source=en.wikipedia.org&amp;utm_campaign=parser&amp;utm_content=thumbnail 2x" alt="Recurrent item" data-file-width="512" data-file-height="512" data-file-type="drawing" height="12" width="12" class="mw-file-element"></span></span> Detailed code updates later this week: <a rel="mw:WikiLink/Interwiki" href="https://www.mediawiki.org/wiki/MediaWiki%201.47/wmf.20" title="mw:MediaWiki 1.47/wmf.20" class="extiw">MediaWiki</a></li></ul>

<p><i><b><a rel="mw:WikiLink/Interwiki" href="https://meta.wikimedia.org/wiki/Special:MyLanguage/Tech/News" title="m:Special:MyLanguage/Tech/News" class="extiw">Tech news</a></b> prepared by <a rel="mw:WikiLink/Interwiki" href="https://meta.wikimedia.org/wiki/Special:MyLanguage/Tech/News/Writers" title="m:Special:MyLanguage/Tech/News/Writers" class="extiw">Tech News writers</a> and posted by <a rel="mw:WikiLink/Interwiki" href="https://meta.wikimedia.org/wiki/Special:MyLanguage/User:MediaWiki%20message%20delivery" title="m:Special:MyLanguage/User:MediaWiki message delivery" class="extiw">bot</a><span typeof="mw:Entity">&nbsp;</span>• <a rel="mw:WikiLink/Interwiki" href="https://meta.wikimedia.org/wiki/Special:MyLanguage/Tech/News#contribute" title="m:Special:MyLanguage/Tech/News" class="extiw">Contribute</a><span typeof="mw:Entity">&nbsp;</span>• <a rel="mw:WikiLink/Interwiki" href="https://meta.wikimedia.org/wiki/Special:MyLanguage/Tech/News/2026/38" title="m:Special:MyLanguage/Tech/News/2026/38" class="extiw">Translate</a><span typeof="mw:Entity">&nbsp;</span>• <a rel="mw:WikiLink/Interwiki" href="https://meta.wikimedia.org/wiki/Tech" title="m:Tech" class="extiw">Get help</a><span typeof="mw:Entity">&nbsp;</span>• <a rel="mw:WikiLink/Interwiki" href="https://meta.wikimedia.org/wiki/Talk:Tech/News" title="m:Talk:Tech/News" class="extiw">Give feedback</a><span typeof="mw:Entity">&nbsp;</span>• <a rel="mw:WikiLink/Interwiki" href="https://meta.wikimedia.org/wiki/Global%20message%20delivery/Targets/Tech%20ambassadors" title="m:Global message delivery/Targets/Tech ambassadors" class="extiw">Subscribe or unsubscribe</a>.</i></p>

<h2 id="Tip_of_the_day" data-mw-thread-id="h-Tip_of_the_day" class="mw-html-heading"><span data-mw-comment-start="" id="h-Tip_of_the_day"></span>
Tip of the day
<span data-mw-comment-end="h-Tip_of_the_day"></span></h2><!-- 3 line spacing of h2 is important for subsection edit links to work correctly, PLEASE DO NOT FIX -->
<div role="complementary" aria-labelledby="totd-label" style="overflow:hidden; box-sizing:border-box; margin:0.5em auto 0; border:solid medium var(--border-color-content-added, #a3d3ff); border-radius:15px; width:90%; padding:1.4em 0.9em 1em 1.2em;" about="#mwt39" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwVw" data-mw="{&quot;parts&quot;:[{&quot;template&quot;:{&quot;target&quot;:{&quot;wt&quot;:&quot;totd&quot;,&quot;href&quot;:&quot;./Template:Totd&quot;},&quot;params&quot;:{},&quot;i&quot;:0}}]}">
<div id="totd-label" style="font-size:90%;"><a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Wikipedia:Tip_of_the_day" title="Wikipedia:Tip of the day">Tip of the day...</a></div>
<figure class="mw-halign-right skin-invert" typeof="mw:File"><span><img resource="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/File:Simple_light_bulb_graphic.png" src="//thumb.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/6/64/Simple_light_bulb_graphic.png/120px-Simple_light_bulb_graphic.png?utm_source=en.wikipedia.org&amp;utm_campaign=parser&amp;utm_content=thumbnail" decoding="async" loading="lazy" srcset="//thumb.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/6/64/Simple_light_bulb_graphic.png/250px-Simple_light_bulb_graphic.png?utm_source=en.wikipedia.org&amp;utm_campaign=parser&amp;utm_content=thumbnail 2x" alt="" data-file-width="717" data-file-height="744" data-file-type="bitmap" height="125" width="120" class="mw-file-element"></span><figcaption></figcaption></figure>
<div class="center"><span style="font-size:120%"><b>What is BRD?<br>The BOLD, revert, discuss cycle</b></span></div>

<p>The <b><a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Wikipedia:BOLD,_revert,_discuss_cycle" title="Wikipedia:BOLD, revert, discuss cycle">BOLD</a></b> is where making <b><a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Wikipedia:BOLD" title="Wikipedia:BOLD" class="mw-redirect">bold edits</a></b> is encouraged, as it will result in either improving an article, or stimulating discussion. If your edit gets <b><a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Wikipedia:REVERT" title="Wikipedia:REVERT" class="mw-redirect">reverted</a></b>, do not revert again. Instead, begin a <b><a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Wikipedia:Discussion" title="Wikipedia:Discussion" class="mw-redirect">discussion</a></b> with the person who reverted your change to establish <b><a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Wikipedia:CON" title="Wikipedia:CON" class="mw-redirect">consensus</a></b>.</p>
<figure class="mw-halign-center" typeof="mw:File"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/File:BRD_cycle.svg" class="mw-file-description"><img resource="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/File:BRD_cycle.svg" src="//thumb.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/3/30/BRD_cycle.svg/960px-BRD_cycle.svg.png?utm_source=en.wikipedia.org&amp;utm_campaign=parser&amp;utm_content=thumbnail" decoding="async" loading="lazy" srcset="//thumb.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/3/30/BRD_cycle.svg/1280px-BRD_cycle.svg.png?utm_source=en.wikipedia.org&amp;utm_campaign=parser&amp;utm_content=thumbnail 2x" data-file-width="1920" data-file-height="1080" data-file-type="drawing" height="338" width="600" class="mw-file-element"></a><figcaption></figcaption></figure>
<p>The <b>BOLD, revert, discuss cycle</b> (<b>BRD</b>) is an optional method of reaching consensus. It can sometimes be useful for identifying objections, keeping discussion moving forward and helping to break deadlocks. Care and diplomacy should be exercised. Some editors will see any reversion as a challenge, so be considerate and patient.</p>

<p>BRD doesn't work well in all situations.  It is ideally suited to disputes that involve only a small number of people, all of whom are interested in making progress.  There are other options, and some more suitable for other situations.</p>

<style data-mw-deduplicate="TemplateStyles:r1034237262" typeof="mw:Extension/templatestyles" about="#mwt40" data-mw="{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;templatestyles&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{&quot;src&quot;:&quot;Stack/styles.css&quot;}}">.mw-parser-output .stack{box-sizing:border-box}.mw-parser-output .stack>div{margin:1px;overflow:hidden}@media all and (min-width:720px){.mw-parser-output .stack-clear-left{float:left;clear:left}.mw-parser-output .stack-clear-right{float:right;clear:right}.mw-parser-output .stack-left{float:left}.mw-parser-output .stack-right{float:right}.mw-parser-output .stack-margin-clear-left{float:left;clear:left;margin-right:1em}.mw-parser-output .stack-margin-clear-right{float:right;clear:right;margin-left:1em}.mw-parser-output .stack-margin-left{float:left;margin-right:1em}.mw-parser-output .stack-margin-right{float:right;margin-left:1em}}</style><div class="stack mw-stack stack-left"><div><div style="font-size: 85%"><span class="plainlinks clickbutton"><a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Wikipedia:Tip_of_the_day/September_26" title="Wikipedia:Tip of the day/September 26"><span class="mw-ui-button">Prior tip</span></a></span><span typeof="mw:Entity">&nbsp;</span><span typeof="mw:Entity">–</span><span typeof="mw:Entity">&nbsp;</span><span class="plainlinks clickbutton"><a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Wikipedia:Tips" title="Wikipedia:Tips"><span class="mw-ui-button">Tips library</span></a></span><span typeof="mw:Entity">&nbsp;</span><span typeof="mw:Entity">–</span><span typeof="mw:Entity">&nbsp;</span><span class="plainlinks clickbutton"><a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Wikipedia:Tip_of_the_day/September_28" title="Wikipedia:Tip of the day/September 28"><span class="mw-ui-button">Next tip</span></a></span></div></div></div>
<span class="mw-empty-elt"><link rel="mw-deduplicated-inline-style" href="mw-data:TemplateStyles:r1034237262" about="#mwt41" typeof="mw:Extension/templatestyles" data-mw="{&quot;name&quot;:&quot;templatestyles&quot;,&quot;attrs&quot;:{&quot;src&quot;:&quot;Stack/styles.css&quot;}}"></span><div class="stack mw-stack stack-right"><div>Read more:</div><div><b><a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Wikipedia:BOLD,_revert,_discuss_cycle#Alternatives" title="Wikipedia:BOLD, revert, discuss cycle">BRD Alternatives</a></b><span class="nowrap"><span typeof="mw:Entity">&nbsp;</span><span typeof="mw:Entity">&nbsp;</span></span></div></div><div style="clear:both;" class=""></div>
